defined_text = {
	name = GetStateName_1
	text = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = allowrenaming_flag } }
		localization_key = STATE_1 #Corsica
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { is_french_speaking_tag = yes }
		localization_key = STATE_1_FRENCH #Corse
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_russian_speaking_tag = yes
				is_german_speaking_tag = yes
			}
		}
		localization_key = STATE_1_RUSSIAN
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { is_spanish_speaking_tag = yes }
		localization_key = STATE_1_SPANISH
	}	
	text = {
		trigger = { is_basque = yes }
		localization_key = STATE_1_BASQUE
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { is_chinese_speaking_tag = yes }
		localization_key = STATE_1_CHINESE
	}
	text = {
		localization_key = STATE_1 #Corsica
	}
}
